### Page 3

3
Cathay’s View市場策略思維
市場焦點
1. 市場焦點:
• 中東戰火升溫, WTI高位震盪: 伊朗加大船隻攻擊, 胡塞攻擊沙國, WTI一度站上百元大關。傳聞俄烏露
出談判曙光, 油價自高點回落, 但運費高漲, 研判油價仍將高位整理。
• 聯準會10月可望維持利率不變, 美債利率保持高位: 中東局勢升溫與AI融資疑慮升高, 資金流入美債避
險, 加上官員釋出觀望訊號, 美債殖利率自高點回落, 但通膨壓力仍在, 使美債利率高檔震盪。
• AI需求依舊強勁, 但企業獲利動能高峰已過: 超微電腦、SpaceX及Meta均釋出AI需求強勁的訊號, 緩解
AI泡沫疑慮, 不過, 考量獲利增速高峰已過, 可分散佈局, 降低市場波動壓力。
2. 後續看點:
• 3大CSP財報(10/27-10/28)、FOMC利率會議(10/29)、ECB政策會議(10/29)、台灣GDP(10/30)
策略思維
1. 股市−多頭不變, 但高利率增添評價波動: 殖利率高位, 評價面持續承壓, 使股價落後基本面表現。不過, 企業

營收獲利未來成長空間仍大, 股市仍有高點可期, 惟獲利成長高峰已過, 盡量分散各產業。
2. 債市−長端不確定性仍高, 宜以中短天期或公司債為佳: 通膨高於預期, 加上政府債務壓力, 長率仍有上行壓
力, 建議控制債券部位存續期間, 選擇短天期或浮動債較佳。
3. 匯市−美元走揚與股市震盪, 引發亞幣走弱: 美元雙率走高提高外資賣超亞股, 亞幣偏弱看待。

主要中東油國產油持續回升 航運運費居高不下
原油｜主要中東油國產油雖持續回升, 但航運運費居高不下、墊高原油成本, 讓油價難明顯回落
• 中東主要產油國的原油生產逐步修復 , 包括沙烏地阿拉伯 、阿聯等國生產持續回升 。這意味著全球原油供給端的
緊縮壓力已較上個季度改善。從供需平衡的角度來看, 產油回升理應為油價帶來下行空間。
• 然而, 運輸環節卻抵消上述供給改善的影響。中東航道的安全風險持續存在,部分油輪則改道好望角, 種種因素均推
升運費水準。高昂的航運成本成為原油到岸價格的重要墊高因素, 使得即使產地價格出現鬆動, 最終到達消費地的
成本仍居高不下。這種「產地供給改善、運抵成本居高」落差, 讓市場難以單純透過產量回升來消化價格壓力。
• 供給回升與運費高漲、地緣風險難解的交互作用下, 原油價格較難明顯回落。油價可能延續高位震盪, 並對全球通
膨預期與央行政策形成牽制。

資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。

中東戰爭以來, 中國原油進口下降 今年以來, 中國油品與塑料出口價格走升
原油｜中國原油進口下降, 雖有助舒緩原油供需緊俏, 但禁止油品出口, 卻讓需求國油品價格難降
• 中國作為全球最大原油進口國 , 中東戰爭以來進口量有所回落 (因先前低油價時期購入大量被制裁原油 , 積累高達
12~14億桶庫存, 因此戰後選擇不當高價油的接受者), 為原油供需緊張局面注入一絲緩解空間, 並在一定程度上抑
制原油現貨價格的上行動能。從宏觀供需平衡來看, 這本應為原油市場帶來溫和的修正空間。
• 然而, 中國10月實施油品出口限制, 在下游成品環節形成不同影響。受到戰爭影響, 中東部分煉油、裂解產能受到
影響, 加上美國產輕甜油但多數煉油與裂解產能適合以重油提煉, 使得對煉油價差、油品等報價難降。雪上加霜的

是, 中國10月初宣布, 因應中東局勢與國內庫存偏低, 暫停成品油外銷, 使整體成品油市場更形緊俏。
• 整體來說,「原油轉鬆、成品仍緊」的結構性背離持續, 對消費者能源價格的僵固性將更為明顯, 也對仰賴進口成品
油的經濟體影響更為艱難。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。

Cathay’s View
短線升息時點遞延, 後續緊縮方向未改 預估10月維持不變, 今年底、明年Q1各升息一碼
資料來源: Bloomberg, 國泰世華銀行整理, 截至2026/10/16。 資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 截至2026/10/20。
• 10月FOMC料按兵不動, 未來將進一步緊縮: 9月升息一碼後, 近期Fed官員普遍認為無須急於再次升息, Fed將持續
觀察貨幣政策對物價控制、金融狀況的影響; 惟後續升息路徑尚未結束。
• 能源衝擊外溢, 物價壓力支撐後續升息路徑: 美國9月CPI月增0.6%、PPI月增1.3%, 能源供應短缺的衝擊逐步擴散。
褐皮書亦顯示, 柴油、燃料成本正沿著供應鏈傳導, 使原物料、關稅、保險成本持續推升企業價格壓力。
• 短線升息預期降溫, 反映時點遞延: 預料12月升息一碼, 明年Q1亦保留再升一碼空間; 而明年3月將受高基期影響,
有助通膨數據(年增)呈現降溫。
FOMC前瞻｜預期10月Fed按兵不動, 年底升息可能性仍高

官員 評論
Kevin Warsh
(主席, 10/16)
不預判10、12月路徑, 透過任務小組以研議政策框架 。
重申物價穩定仍為核心, 同時關注AI投資需求帶來的
通膨、金融穩定風險。
Beth Hammack
(克里夫蘭, 10/16) 金融狀況大致保持中性 , 若通膨惡化將需更積極升息 。
Mary Daly
(舊金山, 10/15) 反覆價格衝擊將滲透至通膨預期 , 通膨風險偏向上行 。
Michelle Bowman
(副主席, 10/14)
通膨仍高於目標, 9月升息後無需急於行動, 可先觀察
金融環境的緊縮程度。
Thomas Barkin
(里奇蒙, 10/14) CPI仍高於理想水準, 需更多數據確認, 但不預設立場。
John Williams
(紐約, 10/13) 年內再度升息為合適, 但9月升息後無需急於行動。

殖利率創近年新高後, 長債買盤回溫、曲線趨平 財政赤字惡化、公債供給壓力延續
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/14。
• 10月升息預期降溫, 前期債市賣壓暫獲舒緩: 先前較激進的升息路徑逐漸修正, 以及資金對美債的避險偏好回升, 帶
動殖利率自高點回落。另方面, 隨殖利率升至相對高檔, 亦增添債券配置吸引力: 10/9拍賣10年期美國公債需求強
勁, 投標倍數2.77倍創近十年來高點, 並且直接投標人、間接投標人合計獲配比例97.4%創史上新高。
• 縱使近期買盤回流, 但整體「利率高原」格局未改: 1)中東衝突與原油供給風險, 使通膨及Fed升息路徑仍具不確定
性; 2)美國財政赤字、公債供給壓力延續, 加上AI投資推升企業發債需求, 結構性因素仍限制長端利率下行空間。
利率｜債市賣壓漸緩、10月升息預期降溫, 殖利率回落; 惟利率高原格局未改

Cathay’s View
市場評估AI融資風險, 近期信用利差再度走擴 槓桿率較高AI業者利率走升
註: 圖表以2026/1=100標準化, 目前利差IG=86bps、HY=332bps、CCC=1085bps。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。
註: 槓桿率採Net Debt/EBITDA, CoreWeave約為9.5x, Oracle為3.8x, Hyperscalers僅介於
-0.7x~0.7x。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。
• 近期公債利率快速下滑, 信用債利率同步回落, 惟降幅相對有限導致信用利差走擴。另方面, 在融資成本升高下, 市
場持續檢視AI業者的ROI, 以及營收、現金流能否覆蓋龐大Capex及利息支出, 成為信用風險的定價關鍵。
• 壓力集中於高槓桿業者: 大型Hyperscalers具較強內部現金流, 近期壓力集中在高度依賴外部融資、槓桿率更高的
業者; CoreWeave信用疑慮升高(債務規模龐大、Q2淨虧損), Oracle FCF承壓、市場亦擔憂降評風險。

• AI投資的雙向信用風險: AI Capex維持高檔, 持續推升企業發債與融資需求; 但更大尾端風險在於, 若Hyperscalers
下修資本支出並減少外部算力需求, 雖有助改善自身FCF, 但將削弱Neocloud、資料中心等租賃算力業者的訂單。
• 債市布局: 高利率提升債券配置吸引力, 惟發行人分化加劇, 體質仍以IG為主、報酬著眼於穩定息收。
信用債｜信用利差走擴, AI融資成本加劇信用分化

### Page 10

Cathay’s View
主要企業財報狀況
註: 超微電腦目前只公布財報初稿, 完成財報將於11/3公布。
資料來源: Bloomberg, , 國泰世華銀行整理, 截至2026Q3財報。
• 受到油價走揚、CoreWeave融資利率飆升等因素影響, 市場對AI泡沫的疑慮升溫, 科技股明顯回檔。不過, 隨著主
要科技企業陸續公布財報, 包括超微電腦、SpaceX 及 Meta 均釋出 AI 需求強勁的訊號, 帶動科技股逐步回升。
• 其中, SpaceX 本季營收達161億美元, 年增161%, 主要成長動能來自AI業務的爆發式成長, 營收從去年同期的8.1
億美元大幅攀升至75.6億美元, 甚至超越Starlink的連網業務。此外, 美國AI伺服器大廠超微電腦也受惠於強勁的
AI需求, 最新季度營收初步預估中位數達190億美元, 顯示 AI基礎建設需求仍具強勁動能。
日期 公司 25Q3EPS 26Q2EPS 26Q3 EPS 重要內容
10/21 SpaceX -0.36 0.15 0.36 Q3營收為145億美元, 年增率達161%, 此外, 從前季虧損5.4億美元, 轉為獲利 44億美元。人工智慧業務營收從去年8.1億美元跳升至75.6億美元

10/21 超微電腦 0.26 1.62 -- 最新財報初值, 預估營收中位數190億美元, 遠高於預期值150億美元, 年增率上看279%, 同時毛利率從去年同期9.3%提高至12.5%。
10/22 Meta 1.05 6.18 3.83 營收成長26%, 優於預期, 但法律和解費用, 獲利大幅下滑。AI改善核心業務, 曝光率與平均廣告價提升, 小幅調升今年資本支出至1450億美元。
10/22 德州儀器 1.48 2.14 2.62 本季營收60.55億美元,  年增27%, 高於市場預期, 其中, 工業需求的類比晶片成長32%至49億美元為成長主力。
10/22 特斯拉 0.39 0.30 0.48 Q3營收280.5億美元、EPS為0.48美元, 均優於預估, 但核心電動車營收年減3%, 使得整體營收持平。
股市｜AI相關企業營收強勁, 消除部分AI泡沫疑慮

### Page 11

11
Cathay’s View
獲利動能變動下的標普500表現 獲利動能變化下各類股表現
註: 標普500企業當季獲利年增率若高於上季, 表示加速, 反之為減速。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 從2000Q1-2026Q2。
註: 標普500企業當季獲利年增率若高於上季, 表示加速, 反之為減速。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 從2000Q1-2026Q2。
• 標普500企業財報陸續開跑, 銀行股已率先公布財報, 整體表現大致符合市場預期, 接下來市場焦點將轉向10月底
陸續公布財報的大型雲端服務供應商(CSP)。受惠於AI熱潮延續, 市場預期標普500企業Q3獲利仍將維持亮眼表現,
年增率可望達到30%。
• 值得留意的是, 儘管AI熱潮持續, 但隨著獲利基期墊高, 企業獲利成長動能的高峰可能已過, 後續增速恐難超越Q2
的50%。從過去經驗來看, 當企業獲利成長逐步放緩, 股市未來報酬率也可能隨之下降。此外, 在高利率環境下, 股
市波動風險仍不容忽視, 投資配置不妨適度提高防禦型類股比重, 包括核心消費與公用事業, 以提升投資組合的防

禦能力。
股市｜企業獲利維持成長支持股市走揚, 但增速放緩下可提高防禦類股
正成長&加速 正成長但減速 負成長但改善 負成長&惡化

資料來源: 經濟部, 截至2026/9。 資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 截至2026/10/21。
• 9月外銷訂單依然高成長, 年增率62.4%, 資通訊產品年增87%、電子產品年增71%, 接單金額保持高速成長, AI硬
體需求動能仍強, 帶動台灣全年出口挑戰突破9000億美元。
• 殖利率上升不僅影響美股評價面, 也影響台股估值, 加上明年獲利成長減速的擔憂, 進一步拖累台股評價面, 台股預
估本益比回落至16倍。不過, AI硬體需求展望無虞, 2027年預估台股獲利逾三成, 即使評價面持平, 獲利成長仍能
帶動股市上漲。

記憶體｜回顧過去30年十次記憶體循環, 股價約領先獲利峰值一季見頂
資料來源: Bloomberg, 國泰世華銀行整理, 以上數據均為美光財報與股價表現, 截至2026/10/19。
• 根據歷史規律, 即便獲利成長率已見頂, 若獲利絕對金額仍能持續成長, 記憶體股價仍能夠持續創高。然而, 一旦獲

利金額見頂, 該波週期股價漲勢多半提前結束。過去週期股價平均約領先獲利3個月見頂, 換言之, 市場目前的定價
隱含記憶體廠的獲利將於2026Q3至Q4後開始季減。
成長率見頂, 漲勢仍有可能延續很久 獲利一旦見頂, 漲勢提前結束

記憶體｜2027供給新增有限, 若AI需求並未放緩, 供不應求格局暫難扭轉
• 然而, 美光先前給出的樂觀展望與市場的預期出現明顯落差, 公司稱2027與2028的供需情形將較2026年更加嚴峻,
且FY27第一季度(2026/11)的毛利率將是FY27全年毛利率的地板 。另一方面, 目前記憶體合約價與現貨價仍持續
上漲, 需求並未出現明顯轉折。
• 即便2028年以後的AI需求是否放緩, 記憶體供不應求情形是否改變的確仍具備不確定性 , 但2027年各廠商新增產
能有限, 同時算力需求仍然強勁, 明年記憶體廠商獲利出現衰退的機率較低, 近期的劇烈回檔更偏向情緒驅動而非
基本面改變。

日本出口維持雙位數, 惟經濟表現略有放緩 BOJ料維持緩步升息
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/9。 資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 截至2026/10/21。
• 日本9月出口年增16.7%, 連13個月維持正成長, 不過9月出口增速連兩月放緩, 也低於市場預期的20.1%, 搭配花旗
日本經濟驚奇指數自高檔回落, 顯示經濟成長動能略有放緩, 但仍呈現穩健成長。
• 近期高市首相在國會接受質詢時否認政策導致日圓疲弱和推高日本利率, 表示會適當地控制政府發債規模, 日本10
年債殖利率自3.1%高點回落至2.9%下方, 但仍是近30年來最高水準, 且央行總裁植田和男維持穩健升息論調, 預料
將於12月再度升息, 使日債殖利率維持相對高檔。

法國民眾關注焦點 法國信用持續惡化
資料來源: Bloomberg, IPSOS, 截至2026/9。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/21。
歐洲｜民怨烽火且預算卡關, 法債難平
• 法國政府試圖推動溫和減赤 , 卻在極左與極右陣營反對下面臨極高的倒閣危機 。信評機構穆迪於10月23日公布的
評級審查, 預料法國原有的「Aa3」評等面臨被直接調降至 「A1」的風險, 恐推升融資成本。此外10/27預算收入
案若再拖延, 法國10年期公債殖利率與法德利差恐持續惡化。
• 與此同時, 社會民怨也已達到臨界點, 今年酷暑熱浪重創核電供給, 導致民生管制電價在8月被迫調升2.5%, 進一步
剝奪了實質購買力 。民眾普遍認為惡化的公共服務與斷裂的福利合約 , 導致「深知債務迫切、卻拒絕承擔緊縮 」
的認知撕裂, 催生了10月中旬高達數十萬人的全國性暴力街頭抗爭。高昂的電費與生活成本積怨, 正與政治財政危

機相互綁定, 使法國陷入難以解套的惡性循環, 短期法債風險難以消除。

歐元區交易區間下移 歐美12月會議利率變動機率
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/21。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/21。
匯市｜歐元受事件衝擊後回神, 年底利率變動預期仍是關鍵
• 歐元兌美元跌破1.14並下移至新的交易區間後 , 目前呈現低檔震盪築底的態勢 。儘管法國國會僵局與穆迪即將到
來的降評風險等結構性問題依舊, 但市場在經歷先前的恐慌拋售後, 已對此利空進行了定價。畢竟歐元是集合體且
有德國托底, 隨著政治風暴對匯市的邊際影響逐漸減弱, 歐美兩大央行12月會議的「利差變動預期」將接棒成為驅
動歐元後續走勢的核心力道。
• 從近期市場預期來看 , 歐洲央行與聯準會雙方對貨幣政策路徑的角力正進入白熱化 。以目前通膨數據以及雙方官
員的表態, 12月利差難以擴大, 歐元可能繼續受制政治雜音於低位震盪, 但無序下跌的機率有限, 短期先看重回區間
上緣走勢。

王文雯 wenwen.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：電話訪談

矽力*-KY (6415 TT) 
資料中心與車載為成長主軸

永豐觀點 
矽力杰 3Q26 營收優於預期，4Q26 展望正向，雖短期毛利率較為波動，不過隨資
料中心與車載訂單加持，以及第四代製程平滲透率提升， 應有助於中長期毛利率回
穩。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：研究部預估矽力杰 2026 年稅後 EPS 11.95 元(原預估 11.59 元)，2027
年稅後 EPS 15.34 元。考量矽力杰資料中心與車載仍將持續帶動營運拓展，研究部
維持買進建議，目標價維持 644 元(42X 2027 年 EPS 評價)。

ESG 評析 
矽力*-KY 企業永續評鑑整體為 B 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面
向平均得分分別為 B+等與 B 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2023 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

矽力杰為電源管理晶片大廠：矽力杰成立於 02/2008，為電源管理 IC 設計公司，
產品線包括 DC/DC 轉換晶片、電池充電管理晶片、過流保護晶片、靜電防護裝置
與 LED 背光驅動晶片、能源監控管理晶片等。矽力杰 2Q26 產品終端應用主要區
分為四大領域，包括消費電子(如平板電腦、STB 與 TV)36%、工業應用(如 LED 照
明、伺服器與安防設備等)35%、資訊產品(如筆電)9%與網路通訊(如路由器、手機
等)6%、 車載14%。國際電源管理晶片設計同業與矽力杰產品類似者為 TI、Maxim、
ON Semi 與 ADI，台系類似的參與廠商則包括致新、茂達等。 
3Q26 營收優於預期 ，4Q26 展望正向 ：矽力杰總計 3Q26 營收 72.83 億元
(+21%QoQ，+53%YoY)，落於研究部原預估 3Q26 營收達成率 102%(研究部原
預估季增 18%)，優於預期，主要係由資料中心為最主要推升力道，再者車載因新
款晶片推出與市占率提升而增 、 消費電子即便市場需求較為疲弱， 但因公司新款晶
片推出，故反而仍能向上。目前展望 4Q26 亦維持正向看法，營收變化主要仍需視
後段封測產能的供給程度而定。研究部預估矽力杰 3Q26 毛利率 47.63%，稅後獲
利 11.32 億元(-28%QoQ，+66%YoY)，稅後 EPS 2.9 元，並預估 4Q26 營收 76.9
億元(+5.5%QoQ，+43%YoY)，毛利率 47.8%，稅後獲利 12.33 億元(+9%QoQ，
+53%YoY)，稅後 EPS 3.13 元。 
主要產能瓶頸在後段封測 ：目前矽力杰最主要的產能瓶頸在後段封測的支應，主要
是後段封測供給被 AI 應用排擠，目前除原有封測廠的產能擴充外，公司策略也持
續驗證新封測廠，以其紓解封測產能緊缺問題。至於晶圓產能，則矽力杰因具備第
四代 12 吋製程平台產能(第四代製程平台滲透率由 2025 年的個位數比重 ， 已將提
升至 4Q26 的 20%)，故反而晶圓較能充足的支應，明年第五代製程平台亦將加入
量產範圍。針對 2026 年毛利率走勢仍較有變數，係因影響毛利率因子正負交錯：
正向因子包括公司依據產品與客戶別而有不等的漲價措施 、 第四代製程平台挹注與
產品結構變化，負向因子則包括晶圓/封測成本上升、存貨依據庫齡政策而有損失
或回沖認列等影響。不過研究部認為隨 07/01 已開始進行漲價，理應能消弭部分
製造成本攀升的衝擊，遂預估 2H26 毛利率應能優於 2Q26 水準，中長期隨第四
代製程平台滲透率提升與產品結構變化， 理應有助於毛利率趨勢向上。 相較毛利率
是否獲得顯著提升，目前公司更關心在希冀產能能獲得更充足的支援， 以滿足客戶
的需求。 
資料中心與車載為後續成長主軸：資料中心、車載與工業將成為推升後續成長的主
軸：(1) 資料中心(包括主板/企業級 SSD/光模組)目前營收比重 15%~16%，2027
年 目 前 維 持 預 期>20% 比 重 ， 公 司資 料 中 心 三 大 項 目 比 重 依 序 為光 通 訊
>SSD>MB，其中光通訊與 SSD 個別均為 mid-to-high single digit，MB 比重較
低。公司過往以 400G 以下為主，今年 400G/800G 均已導入，隨市占率逐步提
升，又未來若主板核心電源出貨，更將進一步推動資料中心業務規模擴大。公司亦
表示 PSU 相關產品出貨亦隨伺服器需求而增，公司 PSU 產品包括 AC-DC 
controller/ESD/Boost/ Buck/Temp sensor等，目前針對 VPD 進展還沒那麼快。
(2) 車載營收比重可望由去年的 13%， 攀升至今年的15%~20%， 研究部認為車載
動力來自於公司車載晶片供應品項增加， 每台車的可供應的內含價值擴大， 並且客
戶群攀升，除中國品牌上量外，也進一步拓展海外市場。(3) 儲能與光伏需求，以
及充電樁應用，亦能拓展工業領域的需求。 
維持買進： 研究部預估矽力杰 2026 年營收 258.35 億元(+37%YoY)，毛利率
47.65%，稅後獲利 47.05 億元(+90%YoY)，稅後 EPS 11.95 元(原預估 11.59 元)，
並預估 2027 年營收 341.9 億元(+32%YoY)，毛利率 48.44%，稅後獲利 60.39 億
元(+28%YoY)，稅後 EPS 15.34 元。考量矽力杰資料中心與車載仍將持續帶動營

營運現況與分析

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시_dcb09b7c.pdf

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성바이오로직스 (207940)

Company Brief 
제약/바이오 
Analyst 김준영 
junyoung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 김현비 
hyunbee.kim@meritz.co.kr 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요

3Q26 Review  
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(YoY +18.5%, QoQ +12.8%), 영업이익 
6,401억원(YoY +1.0%, QoQ +9.2%)으로 컨센서스를 각각 9.8%, 4.7% 상회했다. 
기존 1 ~ 4공장 풀가동 및 미국 Rockville 공장 매출 약 1,000억원과 5공장 PPQ 
물량 인식이 더해지며 외형 성장을 견인했다. 반면 신규 생산시설의 초기 비용 및 제
품 믹스 영향으로 OPM은 42.9%를 기록하며 전년 동기 대비 7.5%p 하락했다. 동
사는 연간 매출 성장률 가이던스를 기존 +15~20%에서 +20%로 상향했다. 당사는 
4분기 Rockville 공장 매출 기여와 기존 공장의 높은 가동률을 감안하여 연간 매출
액 5조 6,135억원(YoY +23.2%)을 예상한다.

신규 생산거점의 외형 확대, 이익 기여는 좀 더 긴 호흡으로 
5공장은 2Q26부터 PPQ 생산을 본격화했으나 상업 생산에 따른 실질적인 이익 기
여는 2027년 하반기에 확대될 전망이다. Rockville 공장은 3Q와 4Q 각각 약 
1,000억원의 매출이 예상되며 2027년 분기별 매출도 유사한 수준을 유지할 것으로 
전망한다. 다만 영업이익 기여까지는 시간이 더 소요될 것으로 예상되어 2027년 이
익 성장은 미국 공장보다 5공장 Ramp-up이 주도할 전망이다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원 유지 
삼성바이오로직스에 대해 투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원을 유지한다. 고객사 수주 
문의가 회복되는 가운데 대형 계약 협의가 진행 중이며 6공장 역시 연말 또는 내년 
초 착공을 목표로 하고 있다. 다만 신규 수주가 실제 계약으로 이어지는 시점과 5공
장의 상업 생산 전환 속도가 중기 실적의 핵심 변수로 판단한다. 추가 대형 수주 확
보 시 5공장 이후의 성장 가시성도 높아질 것으로 예상한다. 
PolyPeptide 인수는 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업을 확장한다는 점에서 긍정적이다. 인
수 완료 이후 다음 분기부터 연결 매출 인식이 가능하나 유의미한 영업이익 기여까지
는 최소 2년 이상 소요될 전망이다. PolyPeptide 인수는 현재 추정치에서 반영하지 
않았으며 향후 인수 완료 시 연결 OPM 희석 가능성을 감안할 필요가 있다. 한편 최
근 법원 항고심에서 배양부터 정제까지의 생산공정이 쟁의행위 기간에도 정상 수행되
어야 할 보전작업으로 인정되면서 파업에 따른 생산 차질 우려는 완화될 것으로 판단
한다. 노사 협상은 여전히 진행 중이나 생산 안정성에 대한 고객사 우려가 낮아질 경
우 신규 수주에도 긍정적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 22

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 2,000,000원 
현재주가 (10.21) 1,417,000원 
상승여력 41.1%

주: K-IFRS 연결 기준 
자료: HD현대중공업, LS증권 리서치센터

HiMSEN 엔진 및 SMR 주기기 생산설비 대규모 증설 발표 
- 9/10 HD현대중공업은 총 1조 722억원 규모의 시설투자 2건을 공시 
① 대출력 발전엔진(4행정 중속엔진, HiMSEN엔진) 생산능력 확충에 8,336
억원 투입, 글로벌 전력수요 증가에 따른 엔진발전 시장 확대에 대응하는 목
적. 대출력 발전엔진 전용 공장 신설과 토지 취득, 건축물·기계설비 등 생산설
비 구축이 골자. 투자기간은 ‘26.09~’28.05(설계/인허가 ~’27.02, 공장건설 
~’28.05. ’28.06 상업운전개시, 28년 가동률 30%, 2030년 100% 도달) 
② SMR 주기기 제작 생산시설 구축에 2,386억원(자기자본 대비 2.55%) 투
입, SMR 주기기 제작사업 진입 및 수주 물량 증가에 대응. SMR 전용 공장 
및 설비 신설과 기존 공장 최적화를 병행, 투자기간은 ’26.10~’29.04. 
기다리던 증설 공시. 늘어난 Capa로 AI DC향 수혜 확대 기대 
- 25년 3.0GW(선박용 2.3GW + 육상용 0.7GW)였던 HiMSEN 엔진 생산 
Capa는 2030년 7.2GW(선박용 3.2GW + 육상용 4.0GW)로 확대. 그간 선
박용 중심이었던 엔진 사업의 포트폴리오를 발전용으로 본격 다각화하는 사
업 재편으로 해석 
- AI DC 신규 증설 확대 추세 속 계통 연결의 어려움과 CCGT, Aero -
derivative 대비 납기 및 부하상승시간, 모듈화 용이성 영역에서 장점을 가진 
발전용 중속엔진에 대한 수요 지속 기대(260908 당사 보고서 참조) 
- 지난 Corban Energy Group 향 계약 건(1,000MW, $ 674 mn)을 단순 
적용 시 금번 증설의 향후 매출액 기여규모는 연간 $ 2.8 bn에 달함. 엔진 시
장의 Seller-Market 기조에 따라 20%를 상회하는 높은 이익률이 지속될 것
으로 전망함에 따라 향후 유의미한 이익 성장 기대 
- HD현대마린솔루션 또한 LTSA 수주 저변 확대 측면에서 향후 이익 확장 가

Document 233: Anthropic推出全天候AI智能体Claude Duet，Opus 5.5 驱动

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic推出全天候AI智能体Claude Duet，Opus 5.5 驱动.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Anthropic 表⽰， Claude Duet ⾯向⼯作任务繁重的专业⼈⼠和⼩企业主设计，定位为能够
在后台持续运⾏、主动识别⽤⼾需求并执⾏⼯作任务的 AI 智能体。⽬前产品仍处于研究预
览阶段，仅通过候补名单向美国地区的 Claude Pro 和 Max 订阅⽤⼾开放。
摘要：
Anthropic 正将 AI 竞争从模型能⼒延伸⾄任务执⾏。
当地时间周三，公司推出新应⽤ Claude Duet ，将其定位为能够在后台持续运⾏、主动识别⽤⼾
需求并执⾏⼯作任务的 AI 智能体，进⼀步拓展 Claude 的产品形态。
与等待⽤⼾发出指令的传统对话式助⼿不同， Claude Duet 能够汇总关联应⽤中的重要信息，在
需要⽤⼾关注时主动发送通知，并处理从会议安排到财务预测、⽹⻚应⽤开发等多种任务。
不过，这款产品⽬前仍处于研究预览阶段，仅通过候补名单向美国地区的 Claude Pro 和 Max 订
阅⽤⼾开放。有限的开放范围意味着， Claude Duet 尚未进⼊⾯向更⼴泛⽤⼾群体的全⾯竞争阶

段。
Anthropic 表⽰， Claude Duet 基于其旗舰模型 Claude Opus 5.5 开发，可在⽤⼾离开设备后继续
执⾏任务，但在判断信息紧急程度和处理部分个⼈事务⽅⾯仍存在局限。随着 Meta 和 OpenAI 也
在推进主动式 AI 智能体， Anthropic 正尝试将模型能⼒转化为⽤⼾服务。
从响应指令转向主动执⾏
根据 Anthropic 发布的官⽅公告， Claude Duet ⾯向⼯作任务繁重的专业⼈⼠和⼩企业主设计。
应⽤每天早晨会汇总关联应⽤中的重要事项，并在⼀天中持续检查相关信息，在发现需要⽤⼾
处理的事项时主动发出提醒。
⽤⼾也可以直接交办任务。除安排会议等⽇常⾏政⼯作外， Claude Duet 还能够借助多个⼯具构
建仪表盘、制作财务或销售预测、开发原型及⽹⻚应⽤。
其核⼼变化在于，⽤⼾不必始终停留在对话界⾯中逐步下达指令，智能体可以在后台推进任
务，并在完成或需要审核时通知⽤⼾。
Anthropic 称， Claude Duet 继承⽤⼾ Claude 账⼾中的记忆、技能和已连接应⽤，减少重复配置

的需要。产品提供独⽴的 iOS 和 Android 应⽤，也可通过⽹⻚端使⽤。
依托 Opus 5.5 ，连接逾 6000 种⼯具
Claude Duet 以 Claude Opus 5.5 为基础，并配备独⽴虚拟机和浏览器环境，使其能够在⽤⼾的
电脑或⼿机关闭后继续⼯作。
根据 Anthropic 的介绍，产品可以调⽤ Claude Marketplace 中超过 6000 种连接器和插件，覆盖
Gmail 、 Slack 等⼯作⼯具，以及 QuickBooks 、 Stripe 等业务服务。
这些连接器使智能体能够将信息检索与后续操作结合起来。例如，它可以检查未处理的邮件和
未付款发票、起草提醒信息、准备薪资处理内容，也可以协助制作营销材料、管理⽹店或准备
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
286 收藏 分享到 :
合同⽂件。
⽀付和信息发送等关键步骤仍受到审批机制约束。 Anthropic 表⽰， Claude Duet 在发送消息、付
款、签署⽂件或删除信息前，会向⽤⼾展⽰拟执⾏的操作并等待批准。
产品也设置了专⻔的安全机制，以防⽌敏感信息被不当披露；部分⾼⻛险操作，即使获得⽤⼾
批准也⽆法执⾏。
研究预览阶段，能⼒与使⽤范围仍有限
Claude Duet ⽬前并⾮全⾯开放的商业产品。 Anthropic 此次⾸先向美国地区的 Pro 和 Max 订阅⽤
⼾开放候补申请，研究预览期间，智能体与 Claude 其他功能共享订阅使⽤额度，后台检查和复
杂任务同样会消耗额度。
Anthropic 也承认，现阶段产品仍可能误读对话内容或错误判断事项的紧急程度。由于产品主要
围绕⼯作场景开发，其处理订票等个⼈事务的能⼒相对较弱，⽤⼾⽬前也⽆法直接查看其浏览

器操作过程。
因此， Claude Duet 的实际价值仍取决于它能否在持续获取信息、主动判断任务优先级和执⾏操
作之间形成稳定的⼯作流程。对于企业和专业⽤⼾⽽⾔，后台⾃动化有望减少信息筛选和重复
操作，但判断失误与敏感信息处理仍是使⽤过程中需要关注的问题。
AI 智能体竞争进⼀步延伸
Anthropic 推出 Claude Duet 之际， OpenAI 和 Meta 也在推进能够代表⽤⼾执⾏任务的 AI 智能体。
OpenAI 此前推出了 Dots ， Meta 则推出了 Muse ，相关产品均试图让 AI 从回答问题进⼀步⾛向跨
应⽤完成任务。
The Verge 将 Claude Duet 视为 Meta Muse 和 OpenAI Dots 的竞争对⼿，并指出， Duet 整合了
Claude ⽣态中的记忆、技能与应⽤连接能⼒，通过虚拟机和浏览器执⾏⼯作任务。
Business Insider 则指出， Claude Duet ⽬前仍处于候补名单开放阶段，尚不能⽴即⾯向更⼴泛
的市场与其他智能体产品展开直接竞争。

对 Anthropic ⽽⾔， Claude Duet 不仅是 Claude 应⽤形态的扩展，也意味着公司正在探索如何让
模型持续参与⽤⼾的⽇常⼯作，⽽不只是提供⼀次性的问答和内容⽣成服务。其后续发展将取
决于任务执⾏的可靠性、⽤⼾对主动式服务的接受程度，以及产品能否从有限的研究预览逐步
扩⼤使⽤范围。

Document 241: Bank bond supply to stay at cruising altitude in 2027

Source PDF: all report pdf/Bank bond supply to stay at cruising altitude in 2027.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

We expect EUR bank bond supply to reach €450bn in 2027.
Redemptions remain the main driver of the high supply, but
the modest macroeconomic outlook is not expected to push
supply up any further. We foresee covered bond issuance
reaching €185bn, senior unsecured bond supply at €210bn and
another €55bn in subordinated bank bonds to be printed

Marine Leleux and Jesse Norcross
High redemption volumes are expected to keep EUR bank bond supply
elevated in 2027
Variables driving next year's bank bond
supply
We expect EUR bank bond supply to reach €450bn in 2027, down
slightly from the €455bn forecast for 2026. In our view, several
variables will be steering next year’s issuance.
Redemptions will remain the central driver of the elevated 2027
bank bond supply forecast. Nearly €400bn of bonds mature in
2027, up €30bn year-on-year and the highest level in a decade.

### Page 2

The increase is especially signiﬁcant in the covered bond segment
where €186bn will mature, a historic high.
The subordinated segment will also rise, with redemptions totalling
€45bn, up about €6bn from 2026. Of this total, €31bn is in Tier 2
instruments and €14bn in AT1s. The senior unsecured segment will
partially oﬀset these increases, with total redemptions reaching
more than €162bn, down from more than €170bn recorded this
year. The majority stems from senior bail-in bonds (senior non-

preferred or senior HoldCo) with more than €90bn and an
additional €71bn maturing in senior preferred instruments.
Despite diverging trends across segments, elevated redemption
volumes remain the main driver of bank issuance in the euro bond
primary market, as institutions reﬁnance maturing instruments.

While high redemption levels point to stronger supply in 2027,
other factors also play a role, such as the macroeconomic
environment. Eurozone GDP growth is forecast to increase slightly in
the second half of next year to reach 1.5%, still well below levels
seen after the pandemic.
Furthermore, our economists pencil in an additional European
Central Bank rate hike in 2026, bringing policy rates to 2.75% in
early 2027. The higher interest rate environment is not expected to

Asset swap spread levels initially proved very resilient to

geopolitical instability and related economic changes this year.
Despite some volatility in March, following the outbreak of the US-
Israel conﬂict with Iran, spread levels recovered quickly and reached
levels close to their historic low. However, a broader escalation of
the conﬂict across the Middle East, coupled with the absence of a
clear path to resolution, could put further pressure on spreads.
In addition, budget and political concerns in France and OAT-Bund

volatility, as well as broader European ﬁscal concerns, have recently
led to spreads widening again. The combination of French political
uncertainty and the Middle East conﬂict could trigger some periods
of volatility in the market in 2027. However, we believe these
uncertainties will not be a strong negative for bank bond supply.
Depending on the timing, though, periodic widening may make
ﬁnancial institutions less keen to pre-fund redemptions coming up

in 2028.

Our estimate includes €185bn in covered bonds, comprising
€180bn in benchmark-sized issuances, including ﬂoating rate
notes, and €5bn in sub-benchmark bonds. This is a €5bn drop
versus our 2026 forecast. It reﬂects the pre-ﬁnancing already made
by issuers this year in preparation for the historically high
redemptions.
Furthermore, next year’s mortgage lending forecasts point to
stagnation, somewhat limiting any further increase in covered

bond issuance. Besides, with the securitisation package entering its
ﬁnalisation stage, issuers with high funding needs may shift some
secured issuance towards securitisation for diversiﬁcation
purposes. Increasing demand for covered bonds from overseas
investors driven by regulatory developments may also result in
more currency diversiﬁcation, away from EUR-denominated
issuance.
We forecast senior unsecured supply to reach €210bn next year.
This is split between €70bn in senior preferred bonds and €140bn in

senior bail-in instruments. It represents a €10bn YoY decline in

### Page 5

senior preferred supply, reﬂecting both the drop in the segment’s
redemptions from 2027 onwards and the potential impact of the
Crisis Management and Deposit Insurance (CMDI) framework. As the
CMDI reforms are expected to enter into force in 2028, they could
reduce the attractiveness of senior preferred bonds versus senior
bail-in instruments in some EU countries, as senior preferred
exposures will rank lower than, and no longer pari passu to,
uncovered deposits in the creditor hierarchy. The funding cost

advantage of senior preferred unsecured issuance could decline. At
the same time, issuing senior non-preferred bonds could serve the
purpose of increasing the bail-inable layer protecting preferred
senior bonds and their current ratings. This could shift some
issuers’ focus towards the senior non-preferred bucket instead of
the senior preferred segment.
Our forecast for senior non-preferred supply remains stable
compared to our 2026 estimate. While redemptions will increase by

€6bn in 2027 and further in 2028 to reach €102bn, we believe the
impact of higher reﬁnancing needs on non-preferred senior supply
will be partially oﬀset by the lack of balance sheet growth against
the backdrop of higher interest rates. Furthermore, the
outperformance of US dollar debt versus EUR-denominated debt
could see issuers turn more often towards the USD market,
potentially negatively aﬀecting EUR senior bail-in supply.
Lastly, we expect Tier 2 supply to reach €35bn and AT1 supply to

reach €20bn in 2027. This is a €5bn increase YoY in both AT1 and
Tier 2 compared to our 2026 forecast, mainly driven by higher
redemption levels. With AT1 redemptions in 2027 increasing by
€3bn YoY to €14bn, net supply is likely to be marginally higher next
year. In Tier 2, redemptions increase by €4bn to €31bn, essentially
meaning that net supply will only be marginally higher.
As the chart below highlights, net supply in AT1 has been
signiﬁcant in recent years but has slowed this year as the focus has

shifted towards reﬁnancing. As such, while we expect more AT1
supply next year compared to 2026, the levels are relatively in line
with the longer-term trend.

The main driver of supply will also be reﬁnancing for subordinated
capital. We expect a further marginal decline in CET1 buﬀers,
although most banks are already operating at target AT1 and Tier 2
ratios. Still, there are some issuers with substantial gaps in either
AT1 or Tier 2 ratios, which they may look to improve, leading to a

slight increase in supply.
Signiﬁcant Risk Transfer (SRT) issuance has also increased in recent
years and will continue to grow as a tool for banks in terms of
balance sheet and capital management, while it is also growing as
an investable asset class. Although SRTs provide capital and RWA
relief, they do not replace the gone-concern and loss-absorption
capacity of AT1 and Tier 2 instruments. As such, they play a more
complementary role in capital management, and we do not expect

it to have a major impact on bank capital issuance in 2027.
Bank M&A could also be a wildcard for 2027 issuance. With several
large M&A transactions potentially taking place, this may lead to
some capital optimisation and less incremental supply needs;
conversely, goodwill eﬀects, potential regulatory increases in buﬀer
requirements or RWA inﬂation may lead to a slight increase in the
need for capital. However, on the whole, we would expect M&A to

have a slight dampening eﬀect on supply.

Document 269: CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng hóa Sài Gòn - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng hóa Sài Gòn - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-22).pdf
Report source: KBSV (Company)
Date: 2026-09-22
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng hóa Sài Gòn(SCS) 
Thách thức từ Long Thành 
22/09/2026  
Chuyên viên cao cấp Nguyễn Ngọc Anh 
(+84) 24-7303-5333 anhntn@kbsec.com.vn

KQKD 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ

Mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi hỗ 
trợ sản lượng SCS từ 2H2026

Long Thành tạo áp lực lên sản lượng 
và biên lợi nhuận từ 2027, triển vọng 
dài hạn phụ thuộc khả năng tham 
gia vận hành nhà ga mới

Cổ tức 2026 dự kiến ở mức 5,000 
VND/cổ phiếu

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, SCS ghi nhận doanh thu 279 tỷ VND (-4% yoy), LNST đạt 188 tỷ VND (đi 
ngang svck). Lũy kế 1H2026, doanh thu đạt 551 tỷ VND (-1% yoy), LNST đạt 361 tỷ 
VND (+0.5% yoy), trong đó thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh bù đắp phần lớn sự suy 
giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng mùa cao điểm hàng hóa cuối năm và mạng bay Trung Đông 
phục hồi sẽ hỗ trợ sản lượng SCS trong những tháng cuối năm. Tuy nhiên, mức độ 
cải thiện còn hạn chế do nhu cầu vận tải trên các tuyến Trung Đông – châu Á và 
châu Âu – Trung Đông vẫn suy yếu. Sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa năm 2026 dự 
kiến đạt 209/52 nghìn tấn (-7%/-4% yoy).

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sau khi các hạ tầng kết nối với sân bay Long Thành được hoàn 
thiện, tỷ lệ phân bổ chuyến bay quốc tế giữa Long Thành và Tân Sơn Nhất sẽ dao 
động quanh khoảng 75%/25%. Tỷ trọng phân bổ hàng quốc tế tại TP. Hồ Chí Minh 
qua Long Thành 2027/2028/2029 dự kiến sẽ đạt 10%/35%/55%.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng SCS tiếp tục duy trì cổ tức tiền mặt cao nhờ khả năng tạo dòng 
tiền ổn định và quy mô tiền gửi lớn. Cổ tức năm 2026 dự kiến đạt 5,000 VND/cổ 
phiếu, trước khi duy trì ở mức 3,000 VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027 nhằm tích lũy 
nguồn lực cho các dự án mới.

Dựa trên triển vọng kinh doanh và kết quả định giá, chúng tôi đưa ra khuyến nghị 
TRUNG LẬP với cổ phiếu SCS, mức giá mục tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

CTCP Dịch vụ hàng hóa Sài Gòn được thành 
lập năm 2008, hiện là đơn vị khai thác nhà ga 
hàng hóa hàng không lớn thứ 2 tại cảng 
hàng không quốc tế (HKQT) Tân Sơn Nhất 
với thị phần khoảng 50%. Các cổ đông lớn 
của SCS bao gồm CTCP Gemadept (33.4%) 
và Tổng Công ty Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam 
(13.6%). 
Hoạt động khai thác nhà ga là mảng kinh 
doanh chính của SCS. Hiện SCS đang tiếp 
tục mua sắm trang thiết bị nâng công suất 
xử lý hàng lên 350,000 tấn/năm. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa năm 2026 dự kiến đạt 209/52 nghìn tấn (-7%/-4% yoy) dù đã được hỗ 
trợ bởi mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi và mùa cao điểm cuối năm 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Thông lượng hàng hóa quốc tế qua SCS bắt đầu giảm từ 2027 với 10%/35%/55%/70% hàng quốc tế 
Tân Sơn Nhất phân bổ sang cho L TA vào 2027/2028/2029/2030, trước khi bắt đầu tăng trưởng trở lại từ 
2031. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
SCS tiếp tục duy trì chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định nhờ nền tảng tài chính lành mạnh, cổ tức 2026 
dự kiến đạt 5,000 VND/cổ phiếu và duy trì ở mức 3,000 VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

2Q2026, SCS ghi nhận doanh thu 279 tỷ VND (-4% yoy), LNST đạt 188 tỷ VND (đi 
ngang yoy). Lũy kế 1H2026, doanh thu đạt 551 tỷ VND (-1% yoy), LNST đạt 361 
tỷ VND (+0.5% yoy), trong đó thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh bù đắp phần lớn sự 
suy giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga.

Biểu đồ 4. Cơ cấu thông lượng hàng SCS (tấn)  Biểu đồ 5. Giá dịch vụ và biên LNG hoạt động khai thác nhà ga 
của SCS

Nguồn: SCS  Nguồn: SCS

Mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi hỗ 
trợ sản lượng SCS trong 2H2026, 
song mức độ cải thiện còn hạn chế 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sản lượng hàng hóa của SCS sẽ cải thiện trong những tháng 
cuối năm 2026 nhờ mạng bay Trung Đông dần được khôi phục, đặc biệt từ 
Qatar Airways, cùng nhu cầu vận tải hàng hóa tăng trong mùa cao điểm cuối 
năm. Tuy nhiên, mức độ phục hồi dự kiến còn hạn chế khi nhu cầu vận tải trên 
các tuyến Trung Đông – Châu Á và Châu Âu – Trung Đông vẫn giảm lần lượt 
14.1% và 16.1% yoy trong tháng 7/2026 (tháng thứ 5 liên tiếp), trái ngược với 
mức tăng 3.9% của thị trường hàng hóa hàng không toàn cầu. Diễn biến này 
phần nào giải thích việc sản lượng hàng quốc tế của SCS tiếp tục giảm khoảng 
10% yoy trong tháng 7–8, dù tần suất bay của các hãng Trung Đông đã phục 
hồi đáng kể.

Chúng tôi dự phóng sản lượng hàng quốc tế và nội địa trong bốn tháng cuối 
năm lần lượt giảm 4% và 2% yoy, cả năm 2026 đạt 209.4 nghìn tấn (-7% yoy) và 
52.2 nghìn tấn (-4% yoy). Doanh thu khai thác nhà ga được kỳ vọng tiếp tục 
được hỗ trợ bởi việc điều chỉnh tăng giá dịch vụ từ đầu năm, bù đắp một phần 
tác động từ sản lượng suy giảm.

Biểu đồ 6. Mức độ phục hồi chuyến bay của các hãng hàng không vùng Vịnh 
Đến ngày 21/09/2026, tần suất bay của các 
hãng vùng Vịnh đã phục hồi đáng kể, phần 
lớn đạt khoảng 85–100% mức trước xung đột, 
riêng Qatar Airways đạt 97%, tăng từ mức 
87% ghi nhận giữa tháng 6.

### Page 5

Long Thành tạo áp lực lên sản 
lượng và biên lợi nhuận từ 2027, 
triển vọng dài hạn phụ thuộc khả 
năng tham gia vận hành nhà ga mới 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng nhu cầu vận tải hàng hóa hàng không khu vực phía Nam duy 
trì tăng trưởng trong trung hạn, với sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa tăng 8%/4% 
yoy năm 2027 và 9%/6% mỗi năm từ 2028, được hỗ trợ bởi hoạt động xuất 
nhập khẩu, thương mại điện tử và sự phát triển của chuỗi cung ứng. Tuy nhiên, 
sản lượng hàng quốc tế qua SCS dự kiến chịu áp lực khi các chuyến bay từng 
bước chuyển sang Long Thành. Trong kịch bản cơ sở, chúng tôi giả định tỷ lệ 
chuyến bay quốc tế chuyển sang Long Thành lần lượt đạt 10%/35%/55%/70% 
trong giai đoạn 2027–2030, với tác động lên sản lượng thực tế phụ thuộc vào 
tiến độ khai thác nhà ga hàng hóa và hệ thống logistics tại sân bay mới.

Đồng thời, giá dịch vụ quốc tế dự kiến bắt đầu giảm từ giữa năm 2027 nhằm giữ 
chân các hãng hàng không, trong khi giá nội địa kỳ vọng duy trì tăng 3%/năm. 
Theo đó, biên lợi nhuận gộp dự kiến giảm nhẹ trong năm 2027 và chịu áp lực 
đáng kể từ 2028 khi tỷ trọng hàng quốc tế có mức giá dịch vụ cao suy giảm.

Bên cạnh đó, SCS có cơ hội mở rộng quy mô khai thác trong dài hạn thông qua 
việc tham gia vận hành nhà ga hàng hóa số 1 tại Long Thành, công suất thiết kế 
550 nghìn tấn/năm. Doanh nghiệp đã ký biên bản ghi nhớ với ACV về khả năng 
tham gia vận hành nhà ga này, qua đó có thể tiếp cận nguồn hàng dịch chuyển 
từ Tân Sơn Nhất nếu được lựa chọn. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi chưa đưa nguồn thu 
từ dự án vào kịch bản cơ sở khi chưa có kết quả lựa chọn đơn vị vận hành chính 
thức.

Biểu đồ 7. Thông lượng hàng và biên LNG của SCS 2022 – 2028F

Nguồn: SCS, KBSV dự phóng

Nền tảng tài chính lành mạnh và 
chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định 
hỗ trợ định giá cổ phiếu 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng SCS tiếp tục duy trì chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định trong 
những năm tới nhờ khả năng tạo dòng tiền cao, quy mô tiền gửi lớn và mức 
đòn bẩy tài chính thấp. Trong 1H2026, thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh đã bù đắp 
phần lớn sự suy giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga, giúp LNST duy 
trì ổn định ở mức 361 tỷ VND (+0.5% yoy). Chúng tôi dự phóng cổ tức tiền mặt 
năm 2026 tiếp tục đạt 5,000 VND/cổ phiếu, trước khi duy trì ở mức 3,000 
VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027, phù hợp với triển vọng lợi nhuận chịu áp lực từ 
Long Thành và nhu cầu tích lũy nguồn lực tài chính để tham gia các dự án mới.

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu  
 Sử dụng phương pháp chiết khấu dòng tiền (DCF), trên cơ sở cập nhật triển 
vọng kinh doanh và tác động từ quá trình chuyển đổi khai thác sang Long 
Thành, chúng tôi đưa ra giá mục tiêu cho SCS là 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương với mức tiềm năng sinh lời 14.3% so với mức giá đóng cửa ngày 
22/09/2026. Trong kịch bản cơ sở, chúng tôi chưa đưa nguồn thu từ việc tham 
gia vận hành nhà ga hàng hóa số 1 tại Long Thành vào định giá. Các giả định 
chính bao gồm: 
- Thông lượng hàng quốc tế năm 2026 đạt 209.4 nghìn tấn (-7% yoy), với 
bốn tháng cuối năm giảm 4% yoy do mạng bay Trung Đông chưa phục 
hồi hoàn toàn. Nhu cầu hàng quốc tế khu vực phía nam dự kiến tăng 
8% năm 2027 và 9%/năm trong giai đoạn 2028–2030, kéo theo lượng 
hàng qua SCS trong 2027/2028/2029/2030 sẽ giảm lần lượt -2.8%/-
21%/-25%/-27% yoy, trước khi tăng trưởng trở lại từ 2031 sau khi đạt 
tỷ trọng phân bổ tối ưu giữa SGN và LTA. 
- Thông lượng hàng quốc nội năm 2026 đạt 52.2 nghìn tấn (-4% yoy), với 
bốn tháng cuối năm giảm 2% yoy do áp lực chi phí vận tải và điều 
chỉnh mạng bay. Sản lượng dự kiến phục hồi 4% năm 2027 và 6%/năm 
từ 2028 khi chi phí nhiên liệu hạ nhiệt và năng lực khai thác cải thiện. 
- Giá nội địa dự kiến tăng 3%/năm, trong khi giá quốc tế bắt đầu giảm 
từ giữa 2027 nhằm giữ chân các hãng hàng không. Biên lợi nhuận gộp 
dự kiến giảm nhẹ trong 2027 và chịu áp lực đáng kể từ 2028 khi tỷ 
trọng hàng quốc tế suy giảm và chi phí cố định không giảm tương ứng

Document 302: French business confidence defies the turmoil for now

Source PDF: all report pdf/French business confidence defies the turmoil for now.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

October’s unexpected rebound in business conﬁdence is good
news, particularly for industry. But the survey does not yet
capture the impact of the latest social unrest, while higher
interest rates, a weak labour market and deteriorating public
ﬁnances are clouding the outlook further
Charlotte de Montpellier
An unexpected improvement
Against all expectations, business conﬁdence edged up in October
despite the sharp rise in French interest rates since September,

gaining one point to reach 97. It therefore remains below its long-
term average, but the rebound is a welcome surprise in the current
environment.
The main surprise came from industry, where the indicator jumped
four points to 105. The increase was largely concentrated in
transport equipment and mainly reﬂected an improvement in order
books, particularly foreign orders. The other components also
improved, but much more modestly. In services, the rebound was

### Page 2

limited to one point, taking the indicator to 99. This mainly
reﬂected stronger expectations for activity and demand, as well as
a marked recovery in administrative and support services. By
contrast, conﬁdence deteriorated in retail and construction.
On prices, the survey provides no new inﬂationary signal. Intentions
to raise prices declined in industry and remained stable in both
services and construction.
The rebound is encouraging, especially after several months of

disappointing industrial data. But it does not fundamentally alter
the outlook. The indicator of perceived economic uncertainty rose
sharply. In services, it even reached its highest level outside the
Covid-19 crisis.
More importantly, the employment climate fell three points to 95,
driven by a clear deterioration in hiring intentions in services
excluding temporary employment. The signal from the labour
market therefore remains ﬁrmly negative, which should limit

growth in household income and consumption over the coming
quarters.
Finally, the survey was conducted between late September and
early October. It therefore captures the widening in French spreads,
but not the violence and social unrest seen last weekend. October’s
rebound is encouraging, but fragile and already partly overtaken by
events.
Social unrest is already changing the picture
Since the October business conﬁdence data was collected, the
nature of the risk has changed. It no longer relates only to weak

activity and the impact of higher interest rates, but also to the
economic, political and ﬁnancial consequences of social unrest.
The economic cost of the violence is not limited to broken shop
windows and looting. As explained here, material damage
represents only the most visible part of the bill. Closures, lower
footfall and permanently lost sales could weigh far more heavily on
activity.

### Page 3

But the real issue is persistence. A brief and localised episode would
probably have a limited macroeconomic impact, while prolonged
unrest could undermine conﬁdence by encouraging households to
avoid city centres, tourists to postpone their trips and businesses to
delay investment. This could turn a temporary disruption into a
more lasting economic shock.
The unrest also makes it even more diﬃcult to secure
parliamentary approval for the budget, at a time when the

government has virtually no room to respond through additional
spending or tax cuts. France therefore has neither the ﬁscal room to
buy social peace nor the political room to impose the necessary
adjustment easily.
The vicious circle is under way
The reaction in ﬁnancial markets shows that the conﬁdence
channel is already at work. The yield on 10y French OATs has
crossed the symbolic 5% threshold, while the spread over the
German Bund has widened beyond 160bp, from 137bp last Friday.

These moves reﬂect both growing doubts about the ability of any
future government to pass the necessary reforms and savings
measures, and mounting concerns about the outlook for the French
economy.
This creates a vicious circle. At the same time, the renewed
deterioration in ﬁnancial conditions is further weakening an already
very poor growth outlook by increasing the cost of credit. We expect
GDP growth of just 0.3% in 2026 and 0.6% in 2027, but the risks are

tilted towards another downward revision. Against this backdrop,
the government’s objective of reducing the deﬁcit to 5% of GDP in
2027 looks particularly fragile, as it is based on growth of 0.5% in
2026 and 1% in 2027, well above our forecast. Even if the draft
budget were adopted in full, it might therefore not be enough, as
weaker activity would automatically reduce the expected tax
revenues.
A rating downgrade would merely conﬁrm
the obvious

Read more
Download
On Friday, Moody’s is expected to formally acknowledge the
deterioration in France’s public ﬁnances. Our base case is a one-

notch downgrade from Aa3 to A1, with a stable outlook. This would
bring its rating into line with those of Fitch and S&P, which both rate
France A+ with a stable outlook. However, a negative outlook
following the downgrade cannot be ruled out. This would signal
that a further downgrade could follow in the relatively short term.
Fitch issued an unambiguous warning on 12 October. In the
absence of meaningful progress on the deﬁcit, the outlook could be

revised to negative, while another substantial increase in debt could
lead to a downgrade. Its base case now sees public debt reaching
almost 128% of GDP in 2030, with the deﬁcit still close to 5.5% and
the interest burden rising to 3.8% of GDP. An unscheduled rating
action before Fitch’s next review therefore cannot be ruled out if the
objective of reducing the deﬁcit to 5% in 2027 becomes clearly
unattainable.
These decisions do not change the diagnosis for France. They

merely provide formal conﬁrmation of a deterioration in the ﬁscal
position that is already widely visible. They are also a reminder
that, without a credible improvement in the growth and deﬁcit
outlook, rising debt and its associated interest burden will continue
to reduce France’s room for manoeuvre.

15%，反應公司舉債擴建資料中心 ，市場擔
憂其債 務規 模 、 利 息負擔 及未來 再融 資能
力，除違約風險上升 ，過高成本侵蝕企業獲
利、股債皆挫 ，顯示目前即使是 AI、科技信
用債表現也開始出現分化。
► 中東地緣風險推升油價上漲 ，儘管川普宣布

將暫時放寬對俄羅斯柴油出口的限制 ，西德
州油價仍處於每桶 98美元高位 ，推升全球通
膨預期 。虛擬資產比特幣 、以太幣隨風險性
資產同步下挫。

► 美國9月CPI年增率上升至 3.7%，核心CPI年
增率由 2.4%升至2.6%，核心月增率 0.3%，
高於預期值0.2%。能源是通膨反彈的主因 ，
貢獻整體 CPI月增幅超過一半；能源衝擊雖
為主導 ，但消費韌性使企業保有成本轉嫁能
力，住房與部分服務價格維持黏性 ，需求面
通膨略有提高 ，加上企業資本支出提升經濟
與企業獲利韌性，使總需求不易快速降溫。
► 凱投預期Fed 12月將升息1碼，明年3月再升
1碼，政策利率可能在 4%以上維持更久 。受
到油價高盤 、通膨僵固 ，與財政惡化 、政府
發債籌資逢企業債搶錢等影響 ，美歐日主要
國家公 債利 率近 期 來到高 位 。其中 ， 法國
2027年財政預算欲刪減支出(包括養老金、公
部門工資等)引發民眾反彈 ，衝擊主權信用評
等，法國CDS與法德10年期公債利差擴大 、
歐元走弱 ，美元轉強 ，商品、黃金承壓 。法
國政治 不確定性讓 歐股修正 、法股最疲 ，成
熟國家公債殖利率升高 ，增加企業借貸成本

與股市評價壓力，續為金融市場擾動因子。

► 美國第三季財報季展開 ，市場將觀察企業在
高利率環境下的獲利表現 。根據FactSet數據
顯示 ，標普500指數第三季獲利 成長預估達
29.5%，顯示企業基本面仍具支撐 ，其中科
技 產業 受 惠AI 需求帶動 ， 獲 利 成 長預 估達
65%，持續扮演企業獲利的重要成長動能。
► 聚焦科技類股 ，AI資本支出仍為本輪企業獲
利成長的主要驅動力 。高盛指出 ，第三季AI

基礎建設相關企業將貢獻標普 500獲利增長
的54%。此外，大型CSP之雲端業務營收有
望加速成長 ，反映AI運算及雲端服務需求持
續提升，有助支撐後續資本支出擴張動能。
► 近期市場波動仍受高利率環境影響 ，壓抑投
資人風險偏好與估值擴張空間 ，顯示市場目
前壓力更多來自評價面而非基本面 。隨財報
數據陸續公布 ，若企業持續繳出優於預期的
獲利表現 ，並維持正向財測 ，市場有望逐步
回歸基本面評價 ，使企業獲利成長逐步支撐
股市表現。

投資焦點
► 醫療保健 、醫療服務 與生物技術等行業 ，在
景氣不好時 ，市場預期民眾對醫療保健產業
的需求 不易 下降 ， 成為傳 統防禦 型類 股代
表。目前金融環境利率偏高 ，11月美國總統
大選國會恐變天 ，股市波動風險上升 ，觀察
過去當 VIX恐慌指數上揚時 ，醫療保健產業
相較於科技股更穩健，具有更好抗震能力。
► 醫療與生技類股近一年漲幅明顯 ，主因1)生
技股過去一段時間受到高利率 、融資成本及
臨床試驗風險壓抑 ，部分公司估值偏低 。當
臨床數據改善 、或導入AI篩選資料與 藥物，
投資人重新評價未來現金流與價值 。2)大型
藥廠面臨既有藥品專利到期壓力 ，透過收購
取得新藥與研發管線 ，提高有潛力的生技公
司併購價值 ，帶動整體產業估值回升 。3)肥
胖、癌症與慢性病用藥創造長期市場 需求，
創新藥物 (如瘦瘦針 )讓部分醫療公司成長不
再只依賴人口老化，高盛今年7月將全球減肥
藥市場 2030年銷售額預估上調 15%至1,140
億美元，看好此類藥品的長期商業潛力。

醫療生技近年表現佳，估今年3Q醫院客流量年增4%VIX恐慌指數上升時，健護醫療表現相較穩健
市場波動上升、醫療保健表現佳，獲利穩定成長

► 美國醫療費用深受法令影響 ，由於商業雇主
贊助保險 (ESI)和平價醫療法案 (ACA)的自付
額增加 ，以及最高自付費用要求 ，較去年同
期提高 ，使今年第三季美國整體住院人數 、
手術量和醫院客流量均呈現改善 。高盛預估
今年第三季醫院客流量年增4%，加上季節性
因素，下半年醫療保健利用率將較上半年成
長，整體第四季預估獲利穩健 ，甚至可延至
明年，反應在近期XLV ETF股價創新高。
► 展望2027年，各醫療保險計畫普遍優先考慮
透過提高會員保費 ，和要求費用分攤(尤其是
提高會員自付費用上限 )來擴大利潤空間 ，並
持續優化覆蓋範圍和產品組合 ，預估商業雇
主提供的醫療保險 (ESI)保費趨勢，將在明年
加速成長至過去 20 年來的最高水準 。 以
Refinitiv預估明年美國各產業獲利成長來看，
醫療保健和生技相較於今年獲利明顯增加 ，
反而今年獲利極佳的能源和通訊受高基期影
響，獲利增速下滑 。投資人在整體金融環境
波動放大時期，宜以醫療生技股穩中求利。

股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長 ，市場靜待財報結果與展望 ，和對AI資本支出回收影響 。
考量地緣衝突 、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉 ，短期金融市場紛擾不止 ，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度 。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變 ，逢股市拉回時可分批布局 ，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間 ，殖利率偏高環境使 短中期債券提供吸
引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利
差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金

融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。
 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高 ，使金價短期承壓 ，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化 ，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

Document 346: Opportunities from the bond sell-off

Source PDF: all report pdf/Opportunities from the bond sell-off.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Weekly Market Views)
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 14
Status: ok

### Page 1

WS Global CIO Office 
2 October 2026

Opportunities from 
the bond sell-off

 The global bond sell-off is creating 
tactical and medium-term opportunities. 
In the US, September’s yield surge has 
improved the risk-reward in 10-year 
government bonds, presenting an 
attractive entry point for investors with 
a 6-12-month horizon. 
 We believe bond and money 
markets are overly hawkish about the 
Fed outlook, given disinflation is likely 
to return as the impact of oil prices and 
tariffs ease next year. 
 In equities, we stick to quality, 
especially sectors riding the AI-led 
earnings boom. The upcoming Q3 
earnings season is likely to show AI-
investments are powering sectors 
beyond the technology and 
communications services. We remain 
constructive on the semiconductor and 
memory segments.  
 We also expect the US Dollar to 
peak in the next few weeks along with 
short-to-medium-term US bond yields. 
This creates opportunities for AUD 
corporate bonds, Emerging Market 
USD bonds and gold.

Can equities withstand higher 
bond yields? 
How do US mid-term election 
outcome scenarios affect the 
outlook for equities? 
What is the outlook for AUD 
and AUD bonds after the latest 
RBA policy meeting?

Editorial 
Opportunities from the bond sell-off  
Strategy summary: The global bond sell-off is creating tactical 
and medium-term opportunities. In the US, September’s yield 
surge has improved the risk -reward in 10 -year government 
bonds, presenting an attractive entry point for investors with a 
6-12-month horizon. We believe bond and money markets are 
overly hawkish about the Fed outlook, given disinflation is likely 
to return as impact of oil prices and tariffs ease next year. 
In equities, we stick to quality, especially sectors riding the AI -
led earnings boom. The upcoming Q3 earnings season is likely 
to show AI -investments are powering sectors beyond the 
technology and communications services, such as financials, 
materials and power and electrification. We remain constructive 
on the semiconductor and memory segments. We also expect 
the US Dollar to peak in the next few weeks along with short -
to-medium-term US bond yields. This creates opportunities for 
AUD corporate bonds, Emerging Market USD bonds and gold. 
Attractive risk -reward in US 10 -year government bonds.  
The US 10-year bond yield rose more than 50bps in September 
(biggest monthly rise since 2022) and 115bps this year, amid 
expectations of economic resilience, higher Fed rates, fuel -
driven inflation, AI-related capital demand and fiscal concerns. 
Valuations appear close to fair value, improving the 6-12-month 
risk-reward. Bond arithmetic suggests losses of less than 1% if 
the yield rises to 6%, vs. more than 10% gains if it falls to 4.5%. 
Resilient US economic activity, but softer -than-expected 
inflation. September data showed robust US employment 
(jobless claims, ADP) and core capital-goods orders. However, 
consumer confidence remains depressed, real disposable 
income is flat and households are drawing on savings to sustain 
spending. Against this divided backdrop, core PCE inflation - 
the Fed’s preferred gauge - was softer than expected at 0.2% 
m/m and 3.0% y/y. A revised method to measure PCE inflation 
also indicate that annual core PCE inflation was 35bps lower 
than previously reported, narrowing the gap with CPI inflation. 
Markets appear overly hawkish on the Fed. Money markets 
price about 85bps of tightening over the next 12 months. A 
strong labour market (September’s non -farm payrolls data is 
due tonight) may justify one or two further hikes, but aggressive 
tightening after the sharp rise in bond yields – which has 
already tightened financial conditions - could threaten financial 
stability. US small- and mid-cap equities have already fallen 8–
9% from recent peaks over the past couple of months, 
materially underperforming large caps, as energy and 
borrowing-cost concerns intensify. W hile some Fed speakers 
remain hawkish, we  agree with New York Fed President 
Williams’ that there is no urgency to tighten. We see little 
chance of an October Fed rate hike and expect inflation data 
for Sep-Nov determining whether the Fed moves in December. 
Stay with quality equities and AI beneficiaries.  US large 
caps have absorbed the rise in bond yields relatively well, 
supported by expectations of robust earnings growth led by AI-
related investment. We continue to favour quality companies 
and sectors benefiting from this AI cycle. Semiconductors and 
memory remain preferred exposures: structural AI demand 
should extend the memory upcycle, while long -term supply 
agreements provide better revenue visibility. The opportunity is 
also broadening beyond technology and communication 
services into financials, materials, power and electrification. 
Opportunities in AUD corporate bonds and gold.  The USD 
rebound alongside higher US yields offers a tactical opportunity 
to add higher -yielding Emerging Market USD and non -USD 
bonds. We remain positive on AUD corporate bonds, which 
offer a yield premium over comparable US credit. Our 
constructive medium-term AUD/USD view could add returns for 
USD-based investors over the next 6 –12 months. We would 
also use gold’s recent pullback to add exposure, supported by 
sustained Emerging Market central bank demand. We continue 
to view USD 4,000/oz as important technical support for gold.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions 
  Do you expect more RBA rate hikes after its less hawkish 
guidance? What is your view on the AUD and AUD bonds? 
Our view: We expect the RBA to leave rates unchanged, but 
retain the option to hike again should inflation intensify. We 
maintain our medium-term constructive AUD view and maintain 
our opportunistically bullish AUD corporate bonds idea. 
Rationale: The RBA’s forward guidance remains data dependent, 
explicitly allowing further tightening if needed as inflation remains too 
high.  At 4.60%, Australia’s cash rate is currently 60–85bp above the 
Fed’s 3.75–4.00% range. Thus, even with our two additional 25bp s 
Fed hikes expectation, Australian rates would hold a modest positive 
rate differential. We expect the AUD to strengthen once the Fed 
signals that US rates are near their peak, which should allow resilient 
Australian fundamentals to reassert themselves. Key risks are 
persistently high US yields, weaker China growth or a sharper 
deterioration in global risk sentiment. 
We believe risk-reward has become more attractive for AUD bonds 
following the bear -flattening of the AUD yield curve. Markets are 
already pricing in one more RBA hike through end-2027, presenting 
an attractive opportunity to earn a yield even as increasingly 
restrictive financial conditions reinforc e disinflation over time. 
Meanwhile, the positive interest rate differential with the US supports 
the currency outlook, supporting the case for AUD bonds. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  What does the incoming US data tell us about the path for 
US 10-year bond yields from here? 
Our view: The risk -reward in US 10 -year yields has turned 
attractive following their move higher amid incoming US data. 
Rationale: Recent US data has indeed started to show softer-than-
expected inflation. However, market technicals are likely temporarily 
delaying a move lower in bond yields, with reports suggesting the  
rise in yields is creating forced selling amongst hedge funds and real 
estate investment trusts. Nevertheless, we do not expect this to be 
an extended selloff. We have initiated an opportunistic bullish idea 
on US 10-year government bonds this week and continue to believe 
this idea offers an attractive risk/reward on a 6-12-month horizon as 
yields become attractive enough to entice buyer flow to return. On a 
broader allocation basis, we retain our structural preference for 3-7-
year maturities in bonds. 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in the GBP ahead of the UK 
budget announcement next month? 
Our view: We maintain a medium-term constructive GBP view, 
but keep near-term exposure measured ahead of the 28 October 
Budget. We would view any pre -Budget weakness as an 
opportunity to gradually build GBP exposure. 
Rationale: The government’s decision to adjust the pension triple 
lock from 2030 is an early indication of greater willingness to tackle 
longer-term spending pressures, reinforcing Chancellor Healey’s 
commitment to fiscal discipline and meeting the fiscal rules. Markets 
are likely to scrutinise the Budget closely for evidence that this 
discipline extends to near -term tax and spending decisions. A 
credible Budget should help contain pressure on UK government 
bond yields and support GBP. Any fiscal slippage remains a key risk.  
Near term, we prefer measured GBP exposure as elevated US yields 
and repricing towards further Fed tightening could continue to 
support the USD. Over the medium term, we expect GBP to rebound 
as the Fed eventually signals the peak of its tightening cycle. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  How would different US mid-term election outcome 
scenarios affect the outlook for US equities? 
Our view: We believe election outcomes will have a limited 
impact over the next 12 months. We remain positive on US 
equities driven by fundamental earnings growth.  
Rationale: Over the past 50 years, the ruling US President’s party 
has retained control of both chambers of Congress on only two 
occasions (1978 and 2002). All other elections result ed in an 
opposition sweep or a divided government.  
Based on the latest prediction markets, it appears that an opposition 
sweep (both the House and Senate flips from Republican to 
Democrats) is more likely than a divided government ( in which the 
House flips to Democrats , while the Senate remains under 
Republican control). A scenario where Republicans retain a majority 
is least likely per prediction markets. 
While mid -term elections may introduce near -term volatility, we 
caution against overreaction. Historically, the average return for the 
S&P 500 following an opposition sweep is 17% over the next 12 
months, but the average return following a divided government is not 
significantly worse, at 15%. We prefer to base our investment views 
on fundamental earnings growth, where investor attention should 
focus on the upcoming Q3 earnings season. 
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is the outlook for  the memory chip and broader 
semiconductors industry? 
Our view: We remain positive on semiconductors and memory 
chips. Structural AI demand should prolong the memory 
upcycle while long-term agreements improve revenue visibility. 
Rationale: Recent industry commentary points to tighter memory 
supply-demand conditions through 2028, reinforcing our view that AI 
demand is likely to extend the memory upcycle. Furthermore, adoption 
of long-term agreements improves revenue and earnings visibility. 
Despite debates around “Pacing the Frontier”, we have seen a flurry 
of new model releases, along with the introduction of new consumer 
AI agents capable of comparing prices online and making 
purchases. These are early days, but the path is open for a business-
to-agent (B2A) economy where businesses increasingly interact with 
AI assistants acting on customers’ behalf. As these applications are 
increasingly inference intensive, wider adoption should support 
demand for memory, with its share of global semiconductor 
revenues projected to rise from 30.7% in 2025 to 66.7% in 2027. 
Against this backdrop, we maintain our Opportunistic idea on Global 
Semiconductors. Given memory  chips’ cyclical nature, investors 
should avoid overconcentration in memory names and maintain a 
diversified exposure across Big Tech and semiconductors. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  Does a US bond yield stress test raise the risk to your 
constructive view on US equities? 
Our view: We have a target for the S&P 500 index of 8400 over 
the next 12 months. A scenario of a sustained rise in the US 10-
year government bond yield to 5.6% could lead to negative 
returns from the current S&P500 level (7,666). 
Rationale: Our base case 12-month target for the S&P 500 is 8,400, 
which assumes 2027 S&P 500 EPS of USD 420 and a 20x forward 
P/E multiple. A rise in bond yields would have an impact on equity 
valuations, as a higher discount rate reduces the present value of 
future earnings. W e stress -test our target under different 10 -year 
yield and earnings scenarios.  
A rise in bond yields would arguably be associated with a stronger 
growth environment and hence, even higher earnings. However, 
assuming unchanged EPS at our base case of USD 420, a 
(sustained) rise in the 10 -year bond yield to 5.6% would lead to 
negative returns from the current level  if not offset by a further 
improvement in earnings growth . However, in o ur base case  we 
expect the 10-year yield is more likely to fall from here. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist

Document 357: Quantum dictionary, part 1_ Decoding the fundamentals From superposition to decoherence, we unpack the concepts and technologies shaping the future of quantum computing. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Quantum dictionary, part 1_ Decoding the fundamentals From superposition to decoherence, we unpack the concepts and technologies shaping the future of quantum computing. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 9

### Page 1

13030420     1

Transformation 
Quantum dictionary, part 1: Decoding the fundamentals 
21 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• What is quantum computing? How does it work? What is a qubit? And when will quantum reach its full potential? Quantum 
computing challenges our intuition and our understanding of what computers can do. In this two-part dictionary series, we 
decode key terms behind what could be the next major technological revolution. 
• Qubits are the basic building blocks of quantum computers, harnessing properties such as superposition and entanglement to 
process information. However, qubits are highly sensitive to their surroundings where disturbances can lead to errors in 
quantum computation, making error correction a key challenge for building quantum computers. 
• There is no single approach to building a quantum computer. Researchers and companies are pursuing a range of qubit 
technologies, from neutral atoms and photons to spin, superconducting, topological and trapped-ion qubits, while quantum 
computing itself is likely to increasingly work alongside classical computing and AI.

First, let’s lay the groundwork… 
Quantum computing  
Quantum computing uses qubits and quantum properties such as superposition (defined later on) to perform complex 
calculations and solve certain problems faster than classical computers.1 From a practical perspective, quantum computing could 
be used to tackle problems related to optimization, machine learning, simulation and factorization across a range of sectors, 
including autos and logistics, financial services, energy and climate, health and life sciences and national security. 
Current quantum computers can perform ‘just’ thousands of quantum operations. As the technology advances from thousands to 
millions, billions and eventually trillions of operations, quantum computers could begin to have commercial applications.  
• Millions: Financial services and security could experience some of the first use cases, such as threat detection, fraud 
prevention, derivatives pricing, bond trading and arbitrage. 
• Billions: Quantum could help power AI through advanced data generation capabilities.  
• Trillions: Quantum chemistry applications could emerge, including drug discovery, battery simulation and materials 
discovery. 
These are the publicly known applications, according to Oxford Quantum Circuits (OQC). And as quantum computing capabilities 
improve, some of these use cases could emerge sooner than anticipated.  
Now, we’ll tackle the core concepts… 
Qubits 
Qubits (quantum bits) are a quantum computer’s basic unit of information. They can exhibit quantum properties such as 
superposition and entanglement (which we elaborate on below) — properties fundamental to quantum computing. 
Quantum gates manipulate the state of qubits, much like logic gates manipulate bits in classical computers. Despite the name, a 
quantum gate is not a physical object, but rather an operation applied to a quantum system that changes its state. 
From physical to logical qubits 
Physical qubits are the foundation of a quantum computer’s hardware. However, they are highly susceptible to error. To address 
this, multiple physical qubits can be structured together to encode a logical qubit, allowing errors to be detected and corrected

1 Schneider, J., & Smalley, I. (2025, June 10). What is quantum computing? IBM. 
I NSTI TUTE
Accessible version

### Page 2

2 21 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

and making the stored quantum information more reliable. In recent years, according to BofA Global Research, logical qubits 
have become a greater focus for quantum computing companies. 
Entanglement 
Entanglement is a condition where two or more qubits become linked so that their states are correlated, regardless of distance 
(Exhibit 1). It allows quantum computers to process information in ways that can be difficult or impossible for classical 
computers. 
So, how does it work? Suppose we have two photons that have opposite polarizations (the orientation of their light waves). 
Before measurement, neither has a definite polarization. When one is measured as horizontally polarized, the other will be 
vertically polarized when measured in the same basis, wherever it is. When measured separately, they appear to be random, but 
when compared, they are correlated (Exhibit 2). 
The challenges 
Once created, entanglement must be protected from decoherence (where interactions with the environment destroy quantum 
correlations). Maintaining entanglement over long distances is another challenge. Photons traveling through optical fiber can be 
absorbed or scattered, making long-distance quantum communication difficult. 2 Quantum repeaters aim to overcome this 
challenge by extending entanglement across a series of shorter links. 
Exhibit 1: Entanglement is a state where at least two qubits become 
correlated 
An infographic of entangled qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 2: Measuring one qubit in an entangled pair instantly 
determines the state of the other, no matter the distance 
An example of what entanglement means for qubit measurement

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Superposition 
Unlike a classical bit, which can be one of two states (0 or 1), superposition allows a quantum system to exist in multiple states 
or configurations at the same time (Exhibit 3). For example, a qubit can exist in the 0 state, the 1 state or any state that is a 
combination of 0 and 1.3  
A quantum system can maintain a superposition until it is observed or measured. Once measured, its superposition collapses into 
a single state. Another way of thinking about superposition is to imagine a sphere with the north pole representing zero and the 
south pole representing one. Other points on the surface of the sphere represent different superposition states. This allows us 
to: 1) represent complex forms of information; and 2) simulate atoms in a computer. 
Schrödinger’s cat 
In 1935, physicist Erwin Schrödinger proposed a thought experiment to illustrate the strange implications of quantum 
mechanics. In this scenario, a cat, a radioactive substance that decays very slowly, and a Geiger counter are put together in a 
sealed box. According to quantum mechanics, the system can be described as a superposition in which the cat is both alive and 
dead until the box is opened. Once the box is opened and the system is observed, the superposition collapses into a single 
outcome.

2 Rehman, M. (2026, May 11). What is Quantum Entanglement? Quantum Insider. 
3 Microsoft. (n.d.). Superposition.

### Page 3

21 October 2026    3

I NSTI TUTE

Enabling parallel computation versus classical computing 
Superposition is a key attribute of quantum computing that allows for quantum parallelism, or the ability to perform multiple 
computations at the same time. Classical bits can only be in one of two states, 0 or 1. In contrast, a qubit can be in a 
superposition of 0 and 1, allowing a quantum computer to process multiple possible qubit states at once.4  
Exhibit 3: Superposition is the condition where a qubit can exist in a superposition of 0 and 1 
Illustrating the power of superposition

Source: BofA Global Research 
Note: A Bloch sphere is a geometric representation of qubit states. 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Error rates and fidelity 
Fidelity is a measure of how closely a quantum state or operation matches what was intended (Exhibit 4). A higher fidelity 
generally means fewer errors. 
Physical qubits are prone to errors. One way to address this is to combine multiple physical qubits to create a more reliable 
logical qubit. Quantum error correction (QEC) allows errors in the physical qubits to be detected and corrected, helping protect 
the information stored in the logical qubit.

4 Ibid.

### Page 4

4 21 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

Exhibit 4: Fidelity measures how accurately a qubit retains its intended quantum state during computation 
Infographic showing the implications of high versus low fidelity

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
How does quantum error correction work? 
At its simplest, QEC relies on redundancy, or storing information across multiple qubits so that errors can be detected and 
corrected.  
Consider an example: suppose someone wants to send a message represented by 0. Instead of sending a single 0, the message 
could be repeated three times as 000, known as a codeword. If one bit were accidentally flipped from 0 to 1 during transmission, 
the recipient might receive 010. Because two of the three bits are still 0, the recipient could infer that the intended message 
was 000. 
However, if two bits flipped, producing 110 or 011, the recipient could infer the wrong message. If all three flipped to 111, the 
recipient would have no way to know from the received codeword that errors had occurred.5 This approach is known as a 
repetition code and illustrates how redundancy can help protect information from errors.  
Repetition codes are a simple form of error correction that can address bit-flip errors, where a 0 changes to a 1 or vice versa. 
More sophisticated QEC codes are needed to address other quantum errors, such as phase flips.6 Shor’s code, developed by 
American theoretical computer scientist Peter Shor, uses nine physical qubits to encode one logical qubit and can correct one 
bit-flip error or one phase flip error, but it can’t correct both types of errors at the same time. Meanwhile, Steane’s seven-qubit 
code, developed by Oxford physicist Andrew Steane, can also correct both types of errors. It has the advantage of being fault-
tolerant, meaning the error-correction process itself does not introduce additional errors.7 
At what error level might we start seeing quantum advantage? 
Some classical computing systems today operate with extremely high reliability, often described as having "17 nines" of fidelity 
(99.99999999999999999%). By comparison, quantum computers currently achieve fidelity rates of around 99.9%. Gerald 
Mullally, CEO of OQC, has argued that quantum systems would need to add roughly three more nines of fidelity to achieve 
commercial relevance, or quantum advantage. 
Noise and decoherence 
Noise refers to unwanted disturbances that affect quantum systems and can lead to errors in computations (Exhibit 5). It can 
come from sources such as interactions with the environment, electromagnetic interference and temperature fluctuations. 
Coherence is a quantum system’s ability to maintain its quantum properties, including superposition and entanglement. When 
coherence is lost through interactions with the environment, the process is known as “decoherence,” causing the system to 
behave more classically (Exhibit 6).8

5 Microsoft Ignite. (n.d.) Introduction to quantum error correction. 
6 Ibid. 
7 Ibid. 
8 Quandela. (n.d.) Quantum Decoherence.

### Page 5

21 October 2026    5

I NSTI TUTE

To fully control qubits for calculations and information transfer, they need to be carefully protected from their surroundings. 
External factors like noise, energy, changes in Earth’s magnetic field, radiation from Wi-Fi or mobile phones or cosmic rays can 
interfere with the process. Even small disturbances can lead to decoherence, making it difficult to develop large-scale, fault-
tolerant quantum computers.9 
According to QuEra Computing, quantum noise can be mitigated through physical isolation of qubits, the development of more 
precise control techniques and quantum error correction, which helps detect and correct errors in physical qubits.  
Exhibit 5: Noise refers to unwanted disturbances, e.g., interactions 
with the environment, that lead to errors in quantum computation 
An infographic explaining quantum noise

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 6: Decoherence is when qubits lose their key quantum 
properties, superposition and entanglement 
An infographic of decoherence

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Quantum processing unit (QPU) 
Referred to as the brain of the quantum computer, the QPU is the core hardware where qubits reside and where quantum 
computations take place. QPUs rely on quantum behaviors like superposition and entanglement to tackle problems in areas such 
as cryptography, simulation and optimization. Just as a CPU (central processing unit) in classical computing contains multiple 
components, a QPU includes quantum gates (operations that manipulate qubits in a controlled way, similar to logic gates in 
classical computing) and interconnects, which enable communication between qubits and other quantum processors.10 
Next, we’ll look at broader computing architecture… 
Heterogeneous computing  
Heterogeneous computing refers to a future computing landscape likely combining classical and quantum computing, potentially 
unlocking more powerful, faster and cheaper compute and helping solve problems that classical computers cannot. According to 
BofA Global Research, AI and quantum computing could also converge and complement each other, enabling more 
transformative use cases. 
Quantum modality  
A quantum modality refers to the type of physical technology used to create and manipulate qubits. We discuss the modalities in 
greater depth in the following section. 
Lastly, we outline qubit technologies… 
Neutral atom qubit 
A quantum modality that uses individual atoms with no electrical charge as qubits (Exhibit 7). The atoms are held in place using 
focused lasers known as optical tweezers. Neutral atom qubit systems do not require atoms to be ionized, which means fewer 
energy constraints and potentially simpler cooling requirements. Atoms such as rubidium or cesium are typically used. The qubits 
are manipulated by changing the energy levels or positions of the trapped atoms using laser pulses.

9 QuEra. (n.d.) Quantum Noise. 
10 SpinQ. (2025, January 16). What does a Quantum Computer Look Like? Explain Key Parts.

### Page 6

6 21 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

Exhibit 7: A quantum modality where atoms with a net electrical charge of zero are the foundation for qubits 
Key features of neutral atoms

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Photonic qubit 
A quantum modality that uses particles of light, known as photons, to carry quantum information (Exhibit 8).11 Optical 
components such as beam splitters, which divide light along different paths, and phase shifters, which change the phase of light, 
can be used to perform quantum operations. Photonic quantum computers can use integrated photonic chips to manipulate 
photons and enable quantum logic. 
Exhibit 8: A quantum modality relying on the quantum properties of light 
Key features of photonic qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

11 Moore, J. (2025, March 28). The 6 different types of quantum computing technology. TechTarget.

### Page 7

21 October 2026    7

I NSTI TUTE

Spin qubit 
A quantum modality that uses the intrinsic spin of electrons or nuclei in solid-state materials, such as silicon or diamond. These 
spins can be manipulated using magnetic or electric fields, and their states are read using optical or charge-sensing techniques. 
Spin qubits are particularly appealing because they can leverage existing CMOS (complementary metal-oxide semiconductor; a 
technology to build processors) manufacturing infrastructure, making it easier to integrate them with conventional processors. 
Superconducting qubit 
A quantum modality in which electronic circuits create qubits. They are built using Josephson junctions, where two 
superconductors are separated by a thin insulating layer. Microwave pulses manipulate the qubits’ quantum states to run 
computations.12  
Specifically, a resistance-free current oscillates back and forth around a quantum loop. An injected microwave signal excites the 
current into superposition states.13 To enable this process, superconducting materials are cooled to near absolute zero (around 
10-20 millikelvin) using dilution refrigerators, helping reduce thermal noise and maintain coherence. 
Topological qubit 
A quantum modality that aims to encode quantum information using topological properties rather than relying only on individual 
particle states (Exhibit 9). One way to think about this is through the paths certain quasiparticles take as they move around one 
another, creating “braids” in space-time. Because the information can depend on the overall pattern of these braids rather than 
small details of their paths, the approach is designed to be more resilient to certain disturbances. 
Exhibit 9: Resilient to small perturbations but requires special quasiparticles not yet been conclusively demonstrated in nature 
An infographic of topological qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Trapped-ion qubit  
A quantum modality in which ions (electrically charged atoms) are held in place using electromagnetic fields. These ions act as 
the qubits and are controlled by the system with tightly focused laser beams.14 The qubits are held inside ultra-high vacuum 
(UHV) chambers.

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
Global bond yields have risen to multi-decade highs, yet growth and markets have
remained remarkably resilient. But with some pressures beginning to emerge (e.g. in
European sovereign bond markets), can this resilience last, or will more vulnerabilities
be revealed? We ask the IMF’s Tobias Adrian, Moody’s Analytics’ Mark Zandi, and GS
economists. Adrian is relatively sanguine about the economy’s resilience, though
notes downside risks, while GS economists expect only a modest growth drag from

higher rates, but a more consequential one if yields remain near current or higher
levels. Zandi is generally more concerned, describing the US economy as vulnerable
rather than resilient, and sees one-in-three odds of the economy going off the r ails.
Meanwhile, GS strategists and analysts are not especially concerned about banks or
markets more broadly, but warn that further increases in yields or rate volatility could
test risk assets. Key to watch: whether the increasingly interconnected and leveraged

AI investment boom can continue to withstand higher rates.
Tobias Adrian, Special Advisor and former Director of Monetary and Capital Markets, IMF
“The global economy has demonstrated considerable resilience in the face of multiple severe shocks, and I would not be
surprised if it continues to shrug off higher yields, pr ovided they do not keep rising sharply in a disorderly fashion. At the same
time, I do worry about downside risks.”
Mark Zandi, Chief Economist, Moody’s Analytics
